-<section class="band"><p class="eyebrow">2 · Approval &amp; re-attestation</p><article class="card" id="SR-017-attest"><h3><span class="rid">SR-017</span><span>Always-on secrets floor</span><span class="pill approve">approval owed</span></h3><div class="anchor"><div class="field"><span class="k">Requirement</span><div class="v"><p>When a session attempts to commit or push content matching a secret pattern, the system shall refuse at the hook floor — scanning every commit&#x27;s staged diff, message and outgoing range regardless of the privacy toggle, and naming the finding — unless the declared secrets_scan dial is explicitly false.</p></div></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Rationale</span><div class="v"><p>Realizes SN-009 — a credential that reaches a shared branch must be treated as disclosed and rotated, and expunging it from history is a rewrite every clone has to follow, so the cheap moment to catch it is before the commit and the expensive one is any moment after. The one opt-out is an explicit declaration, so turning it off is a visible act.</p></div></div></div><div class="ctx"><div class="ctxhead">SR-017 itself — no cell of this row changed</div><div class="field"><span class="k">SR-ID</span><span class="v">SR-017</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Title</span><span class="v">Always-on secrets floor</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">SN-Refs</span><span class="v">SN-009</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Boundary-Refs</span><span class="v">B-01;B-04</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Hat-Refs</span><span class="v">SECURITY</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">AcceptanceCriteria</span><span class="v">A staged PEM header, token, or key pattern is flagged and blocks; a clean diff passes; the floor runs with privacy-check off, and is disabled only by an explicit secrets_scan = false.</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Priority</span><span class="v">M</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Verification</span><span class="v">Test</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Status</span><span class="v">Approved</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Phase</span><span class="v">1</span></div></div><div class="row"><div class="row-head"><span class="rid">LLR LLR-205</span><span class="pill">Drafted — never approved</span></div><p class="empty">No cell differs from the approved snapshot. Before this table, the two consumers had compiled their pattern sets independently: driven against five samples, four disagreed in both directions - the PEM private-key class was refused at the commit hook (SR-017) but passed unredacted into a committed transcript (SR-176&#x27;s own never-by-value rule, missed by omission rather than by name). The table adds no exhaustiveness claim: redact_secrets stays &quot;deliberately imperfect - unknown token shapes pass through&quot; by its own docstring, and the redactor&#x27;s threshold is DELIBERATELY looser than the floor&#x27;s on three classes (comments on the table&#x27;s own rows state which and why) because a false-positive redaction costs a reader nothing while a false-positive commit refusal costs a contributor a lot.</div><div class="cellname">Rationale</div><div class="diff">A vocabulary two enforcement surfaces must never disagree on belongs below both, on the same test kitlib/spine.py was ruled into existence for (trace.py/spine_rules.py, nine equality pins standing in for one home) - except here the two copies were never even pinned equal, so the divergence this row measures is not a hypothetical drift but a live, dated finding (docs/plans/2026-08-25-remap-alignment.md S8). Component CMP-006 rather than CMP-007 (the floor&#x27;s own component, via LLR-017) or CMP-008 (the redactor&#x27;s, via LLR-177): the module is pure data (stdlib re/typing only, no sibling import) - the same data-only shape ladder.py (LLR-184) established for a shared kitlib module - and kitlib&#x27;s shared modules are single-tagged to their established owner for the same reason ladder.py and stage.py are - a consumer reading a shared table does not own it, and a usage tag would suppress the cross-component seam rule on exactly the edges this extraction creates. Landing Drafted because SR-017 and SR-176 are both Approved and this row must not be read as amending either row&#x27;s own attestation; approving it is the owner&#x27;s act.</div><div class="cellname">TestRefs</div><div class="diff">(see TC-201)</div><div class="cellname">Status</div><div class="diff">Drafted</div><div class="cellname">Component</div><div class="diff">CMP-006</div><div class="cellname">Phase</div><div class="diff">5</div></div><div class="row"><div class="row-head"><span class="rid">TC TC-201</span><span class="pill">Drafted — never approved</span></div><p class="empty">No cell differs from the approved snapshot. The acceptance is deliberately narrower than it once was: it had carried two obligations the shall never stated - transient-limit backoff and stall abort - and an acceptance cell cannot mint a requirement. Both were MINTED rather than deleted, because each describes live behaviour worth keeping: SR-171 and SR-172. An unattended run blocked on a prompt is indistinguishable from a hung one and holds its lane until a human notices. Keeping a separate serial resume path beside the dispatcher was rejected: two authorities over one decision drift, and the divergence surfaces as a run that resumes differently depending on how it was started.</p></div></div></div><div class="ctx"><div class="ctxhead">SR-026 itself — no cell of this row changed</div><div class="field"><span class="k">SR-ID</span><span class="v">SR-026</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Title</span><span class="v">Coordinator resumes headless</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">SN-Refs</span><span class="v">SN-006</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Boundary-Refs</span><span class="v">B-05</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Hat-Refs</span><span class="v">UNATTENDED-OPS</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">AcceptanceCriteria</span><span class="v">A worker reconciles from its assignment + committed evidence and the integrator from trunk history; no information is encoded by color alone (status/phase/type encodings carry a text or shape cue); text keeps readable contrast against its fill.</p></div></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Rationale</span><div class="v"><p>Realizes SN-024 and SN-023 — an accessibility bar left unstated silently reads as no bar at all, so the obligation is stated here even though its clauses were not measurable when the row was written. They since became so, one clause at a time, which is why this row&#x27;s method is mechanical: the thresholds are the child rows&#x27; and the acceptance is their chain. Keyboard operability, an accessible name per element, colour never the only channel, and a readable contrast floor are the charter&#x27;s own subject, and no other lens in the roster names a measurable perceptual threshold — the UX pair reaches robustness (it does not overflow), never legibility. That dependency is CLOSED: the ACCESSIBILITY charter is ruled `always`, so the lens this row derives from is put to every decomposition — and it had already reached this row once SN-023 carried the `a11y` tag, which is why the gap is narrower than the unmeasurable-clauses opening suggests.</p></div></div></div><div class="row"><div class="row-head"><span class="rid">SR SR-052</span><span class="pill">changed</span></div><div class="cellname">Rationale <span class="pill">1% of the words changed</span></div><div class="diff"><span class="eq">Realizes SN-024 and SN-023 — an accessibility bar left unstated silently reads as no bar at all, so the obligation is stated here even though its clauses were not measurable when the row was written. The bar is therefore written from the stakeholder intent rather than from the test, because a test written by the builder encodes the builder&#x27;s own idea of usable. Most of it turned out to be pinnable and is now held mechanically; one clause about a first-time reader&#x27;s experience is not, and it rests on a recorded one-time judgement rather than on a standing re-judgement — the residue named at the child, and stated there as a limit rather than implied as coverage. Fan-out re-stamp: the child count is that same anchor census, not a decision count — this row states one perceptual property, and each anchor pinned to a test as it was mechanized left an LLR behind, so the fan-out rises as the row is mechanized. Findability is the designer&#x27;s — a declared reader and the decision answered without hunting (C-UXD-1) — and legibility-as-robustness is the engineer&#x27;s — real widths and real volumes, nothing clipped or overlapping (C-UXE-2). Both are `always` in this repo, so unlike its two siblings this row&#x27;s deriving lens is reachable today.</p></div></div></div><div class="row"><div class="row-head"><span class="rid">SR SR-054</span><span class="pill">changed</span></div><div class="cellname">Rationale <span class="pill">0% of the words changed</span></div><div class="diff"><span class="eq">Realizes SN-024 and SN-023 — task-level usability is perceptual (is this findable, is this legible), so a test can confirm an element exists and not that a reader can use it. changed</span></div><div class="diff"><del>Unit</del><ins>Integration</ins></div><div class="cellname">Method <span class="pill">9% of the words changed</span></div><div class="diff"><span class="eq">The verdict record&#x27;s two halves, each driven beside its opposite so neither reads as a rule with one answer. THE IDENTITY, pure over synthetic `git ls-tree -r` entries: a listing and the same listing plus a round file, a log fragment and a session log fold EQUAL, while a changed work blob and a changed docs/work/ spec each fold DIFFERENT - so the exclusion is proven to be an exclusion rather than a fold that ignores everything. THE ENCODING BOUNDARY, driven through git rather than asserted about it: a record path holding a non-ASCII character - the shape git QUOTES, asserted in the fixture so it cannot silently stop reproducing - leaves the identity unmoved, while an accented WORK path moves it; and raw listings with one blob renamed from invalid-UTF-8 path byte 200 to 201 - asserted to COLLIDE under replacement decoding - fold DIFFERENT through tree_identity, so parsing and the ASCII record-prefix boundary occur before any decode. THE TRAILER GRAMMAR: format/parse round-trip; the LAST trailer in a message governs, so a rework commit quoting the round it answers does not have the quotation read as its own attestation; and three malformed shapes - a verdict word outside the enum, a 40-hex tree, a missing round count - each fail to PARSE rather than parse to something. THE NAME PARSE: a round file with and without the relaxed tag, the pre-train flat layout, a session log, and the legacy hand-authored rollup answering None because it names no reviewed sha. THE GATE, on a two-branch git fixture with the trunk checked out: a logged reviewer round naming the current tree clears it with NO rollup present anywhere; the same file with a BUILD session log behind it does not (the implementer-authored round); a SECOND round file committed behind that ONE session log, whose NAME claims the other declared phase, is not a second reviewer - both files are asserted committed and the single log with them, the entries carry the LOG&#x27;s phase, so at a declared policy of 2 REVIEW-B is still outstanding and the gate refuses by name until a REVIEW-B whose own session log declares it arrives and clears it; TWO session logs at ONE (train, ordinal) declaring DIFFERENT review phases - both asserted committed, with the round file they would otherwise phase asserted still present so the empty answer is the ambiguity rule and not an empty scan - serve NO round at all rather than letting the last log read win, and the gate refuses by name while the single-log arm beside it clears; a round whose tree was reworked away does not, while a log-fragment and scoreboard commit after a round leaves it governing; a CHANGES-REQUESTED round refuses; and a verified Review-Verdict trailer contradicting the rounds refuses by name in its verdict word and in a round count ABOVE the evidence, while a count BELOW it does NOT - both directions driven, because the writer is best-effort and a stamp the evidence outruns is a missed stamp whose refusal parked an approved lane at the very stop the cross-check exists to prevent - and TWO attestations at one governing tree leave the NEWEST governing so a legitimately re-drawn round is not accused of forgery. THE CARRIER IS VERIFIED AND NOT ONLY PARSED, which is what &quot;the trailer cannot create an approval&quot; costs: the identical attestation words on a commit that CHANGED the work under them - asserted in the fixture to have really moved the governing identity - are read at neither the tree they NAME nor the tree they rode, beside the record-only carrier of the same words that IS read, so copying a true attestation onto later work buys nothing. THE WRITER IS DRIVEN END TO END, not only the trailer helper: a completed round is run through the loop&#x27;s own complete_review_round on a repository fixture and the commit it lands is asserted to CARRY the attestation, to name the governing tree, to be read back by the branch reader under the key the cross-check looks up, to leave that governing tree unmoved, and to satisfy the gate - the whole path between a round completing and its attestation landing, which every other trailer case skipped by committing the string itself, and which nothing anywhere reports when it stops writing because absence is the state the cross-check must stand down on. THE RESUME&#x27;S OWED SET IS THE EVIDENCE&#x27;S ALONE: with both declared phases served at the governing tree and a stale untracked owed-marker still present - the state a run killed between the last reviewer&#x27;s commit and the round completing leaves - the resume is asserted to queue NOTHING, and beside it a genuine rework with one phase redrawn is asserted to queue that phase and only it. THE PEEL: a genuine Bar-Green refresh commit, verified against its own tree and parent, moves the branch tip&#x27;s identity and neither reader&#x27;s answer - the loop still says no round is owed and the gate still merges - which is the one commit class two separately-chosen revs disagreed on; and the same refresh with a coordinator TELEMETRY commit stacked on top of it - a record path, so the fold ignores it and only the tip-only peel could be defeated by it - still owes no round and still merges. THE </span><del>SECOND</del><ins>PRIMARY</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>DISPOSABLE</del><ins>ADJUDICATION</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>CLASS,</del><ins>CARRIER: verify recognition of</ins><span class="eq"> the </span><del>machinery&#x27;s</del><ins>exact</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>own</del><ins>publication</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>MECHANICAL</del><ins>constructed</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>adjudication</del><ins>under</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>close admitted by mechanical_close_attestation, is driven beside it in the same idiom - the positive</del><ins>TC-147</ins><span class="eq"> and </span><del>its</del><ins>exclusively</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>subject/path</del><ins>owned</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>refusals</del><ins>under</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>-</del><ins>TC-148.</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>so the peel reads as</del><ins>Only</ins><span class="eq"> a verified </span><del>ADMISSION</del><ins>carrier maps the final tree to its reviewed candidate; forged subject, wrong WI/turn, stale proposal or candidate, extra/missing path, cross-WI close, malformed successor, partial snapshot</ins><span class="eq"> and </span><del>not</del><ins>unreviewed</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>as</del><ins>decision</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>a</del><ins>each</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>subject</del><ins>fail</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>match.</del><ins>toward</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>The</del><ins>more</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>positive:</del><ins>review.</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>a</del><ins>Construction,</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>mechanical</del><ins>exact</ins><span class="eq"> close </span><del>written</del><ins>transformation,</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>through</del><ins>accepted</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>the</del><ins>effects,</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>composed</del><ins>allocation,</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>close subject</del><ins>atomicity</ins><span class="eq"> and </span><del>touching</del><ins>crash/replay</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>only</del><ins>proofs</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>docs/work/ is asserted</del><ins>belong</ins><span class="eq"> to </span><del>REALLY</del><ins>TC-147;</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>have</del><ins>lock/exclusion/directive</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>moved</del><ins>proofs</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>the branch&#x27;s tree - without which the test would pass on a peel that had nothing</del><ins>belong</ins><span class="eq"> to </span><del>do - and the round it follows still governs and still merges, with no legacy rollup present anywhere so the migration window is not what cleared it; without that peel the close archiving a judged row stales the APPROVE that had just judged it, which is the failure this arm exists for and the one an adjudication lane cannot avoid by ordering, since its round is drawn while the row is still in active/. The two peels are then driven COMPOSING, a station refresh stacked on the close, because a lane produces them in that order. The refusals, and every one of them is driven with the OTHER clauses satisfied, so each is the single thing that turns its commit away. A close carrying a HAND-WRITTEN subject is an ordinary work commit and does not peel; a commit with the PUBLIC mechanical prefix/suffix but a FORGED middle is refused because the attestor derives the actual active/ -&gt; complete/ WI ids from the no-renames git diff and re-composes their exact canonical subject; a close carrying the machinery&#x27;s own subject that also touched a path OUTSIDE docs/work/ does not peel either; and INSIDE docs/work/ is not enough, because only the move itself may create or destroy - a close that SMUGGLED a brand-new spec in beside the move, and a close that DESTROYED an already-terminal archived record beside it, are each refused, since peeling either would measure a spec&#x27;s injection or a judged row&#x27;s deletion at the parent where no reviewer ever saw it. A close whose pairs came out of a SECOND lane&#x27;s active/ directory is refused by the one-source-branch requirement, driven with everything else valid - paths confined, deletions paired with their own additions, the subject exactly the one the writer composes for those ids - so nothing but that clause can be answering. THE BATCH SHAPE is driven too, because it is the only one on which the writer&#x27;s id order and the order the attestor re-derives from the diff could disagree: a TWO-row close, seeded in deliberately NON-canonical order, is asserted to peel to the judged rev, and the shared ordering helper is asserted to reorder them and to compose a subject the reversed order does not, so the arm cannot pass on a tie. All the refusal arms are asserted to leave the gate refusing BY NAME on the round no longer naming the current tree, which is the fail-toward-MORE-review direction every unverified identity-moving step here takes. THE WRITER IS DRIVEN AGAINST THE VERIFIER, not only against fixtures the test file composes itself: a REAL close is landed through handback.close_adjudication and the commit it produces is asserted to peel back to exactly the rev the lane was on, so an ordering or a spelling that drifted on either side is a red rather than a silently un-peelable close on every adjudication lane. THE EMPTY CLOSE is driven where it is OBSERVABLE and its consequence is stated rather than assumed to match the others: a commit carrying the composed subject and a single parent that changed NOTHING - a real --allow-empty commit, since a zero-path diff is the one close shape a fixture cannot reach by writing files - is asserted refused AT mechanical_close_attestation, and deliberately NOT asserted at the gate, because measurement says the gate cannot see this refusal at all. Its refusal is OVER-DETERMINED and no clause owns it, which is stated rather than glossed: the one-source-branch requirement turns it away first, and with THAT deleted the empty diff derives no ids at all, whose composed subject (`adjudicate:  -&gt; complete/ (mechanical close)`) cannot equal the one the commit carries - so the exact-subject comparison refuses it regardless, and there is deliberately no third non-emptiness clause, which could only ever fire where these two already had. The consequence is asserted beside the refusal because they are the honest pair: an empty commit preserves the identity by construction, so governing_rev&#x27;s walk-through step reaches the real close underneath either way, and a test asserting a gate refusal here would be asserting a behaviour the module does not have and should not, since a commit that changed nothing has invalidated no verdict.</del><ins>TC-148.</ins><span class="eq"> THE TWO PEELS&#x27; REFUSAL ARMS are held by tests/test_integrate_station.py, cited beside this module because integrate re-exports these very functions (refresh_attestation, work_tip) and the station suite is where a refresh can be produced rather than hand-written: a genuine refresh attested to the sha it produced; a forged Bar-Green trailer in three shapes, the sharpest being a genuine refresh message copied verbatim onto another commit; an amend that keeps the message while the tree moves, revoking the attestation; the deliberately forged attestation recorded as a STATED limit rather than a defence; and the reset peel&#x27;s data-loss half - an honest work commit merely QUOTING the trailer is never peeled away, and a genuine refresh above it peels back to exactly that commit. BOTH carriers are driven, not only the convenient one: that non-empty telemetry commit, and then the EMPTY commit agent_common.commit_telemetry writes when a Review-Verdict attestation must land on unchanged bookkeeping - obtained by CALLING that producer rather than by hand, so the two cannot drift apart, and asserted to really be zero-path, because that is the commit shape a walk classifying PATHS could not classify and so stopped at, burying the refresh under the very commit that recorded the approval. A WORK commit above the refresh does re-owe and does refuse, so the walk is proven to step through what cannot invalidate a verdict rather than through everything. BOTH ORDERS of refresh and round, because only one of them was covered and the other was broken: the round drawn AFTER the refresh - the order the shipped path actually produces, since a lane&#x27;s refresh is spawned as soon as its worker is DONE and before the merge slot runs, so any slot refusal parks a branch with a refresh and no round - cites the POST-refresh sha, asserted in the fixture to be that sha and not the pre-refresh one, and is asserted to serve the lane at both readers and to carry the coordinator&#x27;s attestation under the key the cross-check looks it up by, while a rework above it still re-owes. THE ATTESTATION&#x27;S CARRIER IS SCOPED TO ITS OWN PATHS, on BOTH arms and not only the one that carries a pathspec: with an unrelated work file staged and the bookkeeping unchanged, the commit agent_common.commit_telemetry writes for NAMED paths and the one it writes for NO paths at all - the call this suite itself makes, and the arm where a scope appended only when paths exist evaporates - are each asserted zero-path with the caller&#x27;s index left exactly as found, so work cannot land under a bookkeeping label nor an attestation ride a commit that changed the tree. SR-Refs reach the same design-row `Module` or whose own body names the same source file. `census_draft` returns the row that cluster would mint, carrying its scope in the typed `Adjudicates` cell and its recursion guard in the typed `Digests` cell (a queue sha over the sorted id/title/needs/safety_class of the ready queue, and a spine sha over the three spine registries). Three refusals, all over typed cells: none while another judgement is queued or active; none for a queue sha a consolidation row already carries in ANY status, terminal included; and a row an earlier consolidation minted neither seeds a cluster nor may be re-absorbed. `parse_verdict` and `close_refusal` read the verdict&#x27;s typed outcome block and refuse by name for any row it moves that is not queued; `restructured_text`, `returned_text` and `edged_text` are the pure transforms the two call sites write back, and `archive_absorbed` performs the absorbed rows&#x27; move to the fourth terminal folder at the mint, where the successor&#x27;s id exists.</div><div class="cellname">Rationale</div><div class="diff">A census fires on a STATE rather than an event, so it re-asks its question on every idle tick unless something remembers — and each ask is a strong-tier judge session, which makes the memory the load-bearing part of the design rather than an optimisation. The memory is a registry cell and not a runtime one so that it survives the judging row going terminal: an active-only guard reads &quot;nobody has judged this&quot; the moment the consolidation closes, and mints the identical row forever. The module is separate from the mint for two reasons that point the same way: the decision half is testable with no repository at all, and the one allocator of a work-item id must stay the one allocator, so the arrow between the two modules runs one way and the effect lives with the allocator.</div><div class="cellname">TestRefs</div><div class="diff">TC-208</div><div class="cellname">Status</div><div class="diff">Drafted</div><div class="cellname">Component</div><div class="diff">CMP-008</div><div class="cellname">Phase</div><div class="diff">5</div></div><div class="row"><div class="row-head"><span class="rid">TC TC-208</span><span class="pill">ADDED since the snapshot, Drafted — never approved</span></div><div class="cellname">TC-ID</div><div class="diff">TC-208</div><div class="cellname">Verifies</div><div class="diff">SR-157;LLR-210</div><div class="cellname">Level</div><div class="diff">Integration</div><div class="cellname">Method</div><div class="diff">Two claims, because the row makes two. THE SELECTION, driven on hand-built rows with no repository: the queue digest moves for each of the four fields that change the question and stands still for the two that do not; a malformed digest cell reads as no recorded digest; each of the two new signals fires where the pair producer is blind and the module signal does not fire on a bare module name; two disjoint overlapping pairs make ONE candidate set. THE MEMORY AND THE CLOSE, driven on a real repository: a queue with an overlapping cluster mints exactly one row and the same queue again mints nothing; a station holding any judgement mints nothing; a queue state a consolidation already judged mints nothing even after that row goes terminal; a verdict absorbing three rows lands all three in the fourth terminal folder with the one-line Deliverable naming the successor and their scope text unchanged, the successor superseding all three, and the census silent afterwards; a cluster row claimed between mint and close refuses the close BY NAME; queue-with-edge writes the hard needs edge and return-to-draft moves the row back with the finding quoted.</div><div class="cellname">Tier</div><div class="diff">Smoke</div><div class="cellname">Expected</div><div class="diff">Satisfies the acceptance folded into LLR-210 (parent SR-157)</div><div class="cellname">Automated</div><div class="diff">Yes</div><div class="cellname">Evidence</div><div class="diff">tests/test_consolidate.py::test_the_queue_digest_covers_the_four_fields_that_change_the_question; C-SEC-2 asks that the irreversible actions an unattended run can take be enumerated, each naming the dial or human authority that permits it — which is this row exactly, for the one action that starts further actors. ONE SR PER (need, property): the loop&#x27;s work-selection invariant — what the next work derives from, in what order, and that no hand-curated pointer surface participates in it — is stated here and nowhere else, so no second row can drift from it. An explicit three-way partition was rejected: the partition already existed textually and still produced duplicated acceptance. The no-pointer obligation is stated for a migrated repository as well as a fresh scaffold, and the migrated half is the one that matters most: a fresh scaffold never had the files to keep, while an upgraded repository can retain the retired authority files and still pass. Fan-out re-stamp: this row states one decision — what an unattended run&#x27;s next work derives from and in what order — and its children are the successive stages of that one selection, not separable contracts; the width is the deliberate consolidation of three rows that stated the same invariant into one, so re-splitting to satisfy the bound would restore the duplication the consolidation removed.</span><ins> The uniqueness claim concerns selection precedence; the admission cardinality here and executable-claim cardinality in SR-156 constrain different boundaries of the same single-WI contract.</ins></div><div class="cellname">Requirement <span class="pill">8% of the words changed</span></div><div class="diff"><span class="eq">The delivered loop content shall changed</span></div><div class="diff"><del>Unit</del><ins>Integration</ins></div><div class="cellname">Method <span class="pill">9% of the words changed</span></div><div class="diff"><span class="eq">The verdict record&#x27;s two halves, each driven beside its opposite so neither reads as a rule with one answer. THE IDENTITY, pure over synthetic `git ls-tree -r` entries: a listing and the same listing plus a round file, a log fragment and a session log fold EQUAL, while a changed work blob and a changed docs/work/ spec each fold DIFFERENT - so the exclusion is proven to be an exclusion rather than a fold that ignores everything. THE ENCODING BOUNDARY, driven through git rather than asserted about it: a record path holding a non-ASCII character - the shape git QUOTES, asserted in the fixture so it cannot silently stop reproducing - leaves the identity unmoved, while an accented WORK path moves it; and raw listings with one blob renamed from invalid-UTF-8 path byte 200 to 201 - asserted to COLLIDE under replacement decoding - fold DIFFERENT through tree_identity, so parsing and the ASCII record-prefix boundary occur before any decode. THE TRAILER GRAMMAR: format/parse round-trip; the LAST trailer in a message governs, so a rework commit quoting the round it answers does not have the quotation read as its own attestation; and three malformed shapes - a verdict word outside the enum, a 40-hex tree, a missing round count - each fail to PARSE rather than parse to something. THE NAME PARSE: a round file with and without the relaxed tag, the pre-train flat layout, a session log, and the legacy hand-authored rollup answering None because it names no reviewed sha. THE GATE, on a two-branch git fixture with the trunk checked out: a logged reviewer round naming the current tree clears it with NO rollup present anywhere; the same file with a BUILD session log behind it does not (the implementer-authored round); a SECOND round file committed behind that ONE session log, whose NAME claims the other declared phase, is not a second reviewer - both files are asserted committed and the single log with them, the entries carry the LOG&#x27;s phase, so at a declared policy of 2 REVIEW-B is still outstanding and the gate refuses by name until a REVIEW-B whose own session log declares it arrives and clears it; TWO session logs at ONE (train, ordinal) declaring DIFFERENT review phases - both asserted committed, with the round file they would otherwise phase asserted still present so the empty answer is the ambiguity rule and not an empty scan - serve NO round at all rather than letting the last log read win, and the gate refuses by name while the single-log arm beside it clears; a round whose tree was reworked away does not, while a log-fragment and scoreboard commit after a round leaves it governing; a CHANGES-REQUESTED round refuses; and a verified Review-Verdict trailer contradicting the rounds refuses by name in its verdict word and in a round count ABOVE the evidence, while a count BELOW it does NOT - both directions driven, because the writer is best-effort and a stamp the evidence outruns is a missed stamp whose refusal parked an approved lane at the very stop the cross-check exists to prevent - and TWO attestations at one governing tree leave the NEWEST governing so a legitimately re-drawn round is not accused of forgery. THE CARRIER IS VERIFIED AND NOT ONLY PARSED, which is what &quot;the trailer cannot create an approval&quot; costs: the identical attestation words on a commit that CHANGED the work under them - asserted in the fixture to have really moved the governing identity - are read at neither the tree they NAME nor the tree they rode, beside the record-only carrier of the same words that IS read, so copying a true attestation onto later work buys nothing. THE WRITER IS DRIVEN END TO END, not only the trailer helper: a completed round is run through the loop&#x27;s own complete_review_round on a repository fixture and the commit it lands is asserted to CARRY the attestation, to name the governing tree, to be read back by the branch reader under the key the cross-check looks up, to leave that governing tree unmoved, and to satisfy the gate - the whole path between a round completing and its attestation landing, which every other trailer case skipped by committing the string itself, and which nothing anywhere reports when it stops writing because absence is the state the cross-check must stand down on. THE RESUME&#x27;S OWED SET IS THE EVIDENCE&#x27;S ALONE: with both declared phases served at the governing tree and a stale untracked owed-marker still present - the state a run killed between the last reviewer&#x27;s commit and the round completing leaves - the resume is asserted to queue NOTHING, and beside it a genuine rework with one phase redrawn is asserted to queue that phase and only it. THE PEEL: a genuine Bar-Green refresh commit, verified against its own tree and parent, moves the branch tip&#x27;s identity and neither reader&#x27;s answer - the loop still says no round is owed and the gate still merges - which is the one commit class two separately-chosen revs disagreed on; and the same refresh with a coordinator TELEMETRY commit stacked on top of it - a record path, so the fold ignores it and only the tip-only peel could be defeated by it - still owes no round and still merges. THE </span><del>SECOND</del><ins>PRIMARY</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>DISPOSABLE</del><ins>ADJUDICATION</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>CLASS,</del><ins>CARRIER: verify recognition of</ins><span class="eq"> the </span><del>machinery&#x27;s</del><ins>exact</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>own</del><ins>publication</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>MECHANICAL</del><ins>constructed</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>adjudication</del><ins>under</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>close admitted by mechanical_close_attestation, is driven beside it in the same idiom - the positive</del><ins>TC-147</ins><span class="eq"> and </span><del>its</del><ins>exclusively</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>subject/path</del><ins>owned</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>refusals</del><ins>under</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>-</del><ins>TC-148.</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>so the peel reads as</del><ins>Only</ins><span class="eq"> a verified </span><del>ADMISSION</del><ins>carrier maps the final tree to its reviewed candidate; without that peel the close archiving a judged row stales the APPROVE that had just judged it, which is the failure this arm exists for and the one an adjudication lane cannot avoid by ordering, since its round is drawn while the row is still in active/. The two peels are then driven COMPOSING, a station refresh stacked on the close, because a lane produces them in that order. The refusals, and every one of them is driven with the OTHER clauses satisfied, so each is the single thing that turns its commit away. A close carrying a HAND-WRITTEN subject is an ordinary work commit and does not peel; a commit with the PUBLIC mechanical prefix/suffix but a FORGED middle is refused because the attestor derives the actual active/ -&gt; complete/ WI ids from the no-renames git diff and re-composes their exact canonical subject; a close carrying the machinery&#x27;s own subject that also touched a path OUTSIDE docs/work/ does not peel either; and INSIDE docs/work/ is not enough, because only the move itself may create or destroy - a close that SMUGGLED a brand-new spec in beside the move, and a close that DESTROYED an already-terminal archived record beside it, are each refused, since peeling either would measure a spec&#x27;s injection or a judged row&#x27;s deletion at the parent where no reviewer ever saw it. A close whose pairs came out of a SECOND lane&#x27;s active/ directory is refused by the one-source-branch requirement, driven with everything else valid - paths confined, deletions paired with their own additions, the subject exactly the one the writer composes for those ids - so nothing but that clause can be answering. THE BATCH SHAPE is driven too, because it is the only one on which the writer&#x27;s id order and the order the attestor re-derives from the diff could disagree: a TWO-row close, seeded in deliberately NON-canonical order, is asserted to peel to the judged rev, and the shared ordering helper is asserted to reorder them and to compose a subject the reversed order does not, so the arm cannot pass on a tie. All the refusal arms are asserted to leave the gate refusing BY NAME on the round no longer naming the current tree, which is the fail-toward-MORE-review direction every unverified identity-moving step here takes. THE WRITER IS DRIVEN AGAINST THE VERIFIER, not only against fixtures the test file composes itself: a REAL close is landed through handback.close_adjudication and the commit it produces is asserted to peel back to exactly the rev the lane was on, so an ordering or a spelling that drifted on either side is a red rather than a silently un-peelable close on every adjudication lane. THE EMPTY CLOSE is driven where it is OBSERVABLE and its consequence is stated rather than assumed to match the others: a commit carrying the composed subject and a single parent that changed NOTHING - a real --allow-empty commit, since a zero-path diff is the one close shape a fixture cannot reach by writing files - is asserted refused AT mechanical_close_attestation, and deliberately NOT asserted at the gate, because measurement says the gate cannot see this refusal at all. Its refusal is OVER-DETERMINED and no clause owns it, which is stated rather than glossed: the one-source-branch requirement turns it away first, and with THAT deleted the empty diff derives no ids at all, whose composed subject (`adjudicate:  -&gt; complete/ (mechanical close)`) cannot equal the one the commit carries - so the exact-subject comparison refuses it regardless, and there is deliberately no third non-emptiness clause, which could only ever fire where these two already had. The consequence is asserted beside the refusal because they are the honest pair: an empty commit preserves the identity by construction, so governing_rev&#x27;s walk-through step reaches the real close underneath either way, and a test asserting a gate refusal here would be asserting a behaviour the module does not have and should not, since a commit that changed nothing has invalidated no verdict.</del><ins>TC-148.</ins><span class="eq"> THE TWO PEELS&#x27; REFUSAL ARMS are held by tests/test_integrate_station.py, cited beside this module because integrate re-exports these very functions (refresh_attestation, work_tip) and the station suite is where a refresh can be produced rather than hand-written: a genuine refresh attested to the sha it produced; a forged Bar-Green trailer in three shapes, the sharpest being a genuine refresh message copied verbatim onto another commit; an amend that keeps the message while the tree moves, revoking the attestation; the deliberately forged attestation recorded as a STATED limit rather than a defence; and the reset peel&#x27;s data-loss half - an honest work commit merely QUOTING the trailer is never peeled away, and a genuine refresh above it peels back to exactly that commit. A WORK commit above the refresh does re-owe and does refuse, so the walk is proven to step through what cannot invalidate a verdict rather than through everything. The convention this replaces asked an author to name the deriving perspective inside a rationale sentence, which reads correctly to a human and resolves against nothing: no check could tell a live perspective from one deleted a month earlier, and a census was the only instrument that ever found a stale one. Making the attribution a cell whose values resolve against the declared roster converts the whole class from unfalsifiable to mechanical, at the cost of one existence check. The severity split is what makes it adoptable rather than merely correct - the cell is empty on nearly every row the day it exists, so a presence rule would red an entire spine at once and be switched off within a day, while a resolution rule reds only rows that cite something untrue and can therefore be armed immediately. Deriving the effective set instead of copying parents down is the same argument one tier up: a copy turns re-ruling a single requirement into a sweep across every child it has, which is precisely the hand-maintenance a resolvable cell exists to escape.</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">TestRefs</span><span class="v">(see TC-178)</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Status</span><span class="v">Approved</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Component</span><span class="v">CMP-006</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Phase</span><span class="v">5</span></div></div></div><div class="row"><div class="row-head"><span class="rid">TC TC-178</span><span class="pill">changed</span></div><div class="cellname">Expected <span class="pill">88% of the words changed</span></div><div class="diff"><span class="eq">Satisfies </span><del>the</del><ins>LLR-183&#x27;s Hat-Refs provenance, name-resolution, severity and effective-set mechanics; SR-161&#x27;s perspective-review</ins><span class="eq"> acceptance </span><del>folded</del><ins>is</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>into</del><ins>inspected</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>LLR-183</del><ins>by</ins><span class="eq"> </span><del>(parent SR-161)</del><ins>TC-211.</ins></div><div class="ctx"><div class="ctxhead">the rest of this TC — unchanged since the snapshot</div><div class="field"><span class="k">TC-ID</span><span class="v">TC-178</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Verifies</span><span class="v">SR-161;LLR-183</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Level</span><span class="v">Unit</span></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Method</span><span class="v">Four halves, one per claim the row makes. RESOLUTION: build a spine whose SR and LLR rows carry a Hat-Refs naming a hat the roster declares and one naming a hat it does not, and assert the second is reported and joins the --strict failure set under its own class while the first is silent. VACUITY: delete the roster and assert the same tree reports nothing at all, then leave a roster that will not parse and assert the same - absence is opt-out for the layer, and a rule that reddened every row on a malformed roster would punish the wrong file. COVERAGE NEVER GATES: assert a tree whose rows carry no Hat-Refs at all exits zero under --strict and reports exactly one coverage advisory. THE DERIVED SET: assert effective_hats returns the union of a row&#x27;s own refs with its SR parents&#x27;, that a parent&#x27;s change moves the child&#x27;s answer with no child cell edited, and that a dangling SR-Refs contributes nothing rather than raising.  copy recording its acceptance - stakeholder needs included, despite their
 carrying no status cell - regardless of any Status movement.</p></div></div><div class="field"><span class="k">Rationale</span><div class="v"><p>THE DRIFT half, separable from SR-140 in the direction that matters: a record that exists and rides its approval commit, with nothing ever comparing live text against it, satisfies SR-140 completely and leaves every later amendment invisible. That is not a hypothetical - it is precisely the failure the git-history derivation had, and the reason a baseline on disk was owed at all.
 REGARDLESS OF STATUS MOVEMENT is the load-bearing clause, not a qualifier. The amendment machinery watches the Status flip, and the sanctioned staged-amendment path deliberately does not flip - so a rule keyed off the flip returns nothing on exactly the rows a sitting exists to judge. Reporting against the recorded copy is the only reading that survives that, because the copy is a baseline OUTSIDE the live file and a walk over the live file can never be one.

<file path="project-trajectory/scripts/primary_publication.py">
"""Atomic, turn-bound primary adjudication publication.

Contracts: IF-180

Contract IF-180: carrier_candidate(root, rev) -> candidate commit or None;
    verdict_reader exposes the shared verdict API bound to that query.
    The exact commit must carry the immutable turn and pass the completion
    verifier's full reconstruction, parent, receipt and author checks. Missing
    or invalid proof returns None; callers retain the unmapped identity and
    therefore owe review. Successful proofs are cached only in this process.
    Replay may create temporary checkouts and run their bar;
    the query imports no objects and changes no primary ref, index or working tree. This is
    a read-only identity query, never permission to reset away the publication.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import inspect
import json
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from functools import partial
from pathlib import Path

from kitlib import verdict

import baseline_snapshot
import intake
import primary_approval
import primary_close
import primary_turn
import publication_verify

TRAILER = "Primary-Publication-Turn: "
# A Git commit and the serialized immutable turn completely identify a replay.
# Cache only successful proofs in this process; restart audits them again.
_VERIFIED_PUBLICATIONS = set()


def _git(root, *args, check=True):
    p = subprocess.run(
        ["git", "-C", str(root), *args],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
    )
    if check and p.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or ""))
    return p.stdout.strip()


def _project(root, turn, candidate, tier="all", harness_root=None):
    primary_turn.validate_candidate(root, turn, candidate)
    plan, refusal = primary_approval.approval_projection(root, turn, candidate)
    if refusal:
        return None, None, refusal
    refusal = _apply_approval(root, candidate, plan)
    if refusal:
        return None, None, refusal
    closed, drafts, refusal = primary_close.stage_primary_adjudication(
        root, turn["spec"], judgment_path=plan["judgment"]
    )
    if refusal:
        return None, None, refusal
    minted, refusal = intake.stage_mint(
        root, drafts, "primary adjudication {}".format(turn["wi"])
    )
    if refusal:
        return None, None, refusal
    step = next(
        (
            Path(root) / rel
            for rel in (
                "scripts/trunk_step.py",
                "project-trajectory/scripts/trunk_step.py",
            )
            if (Path(root) / rel).is_file()
        ),
        None,
    )
    if step is None:
        return None, None, "trunk_step.py is absent from the candidate"
    p = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, str(step), "--root", ".", "--regen"],
        cwd=root,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    if p.returncode:
        return None, None, "regeneration failed: " + (p.stdout + p.stderr)[-2000:]
    _git(root, "add", "-A")
    product_bar = _outside_adjudication_scope(root)
    if product_bar:
        green, _out, why = publication_verify.run_bar(
            Path(root), Path(harness_root or root), tier
        )
        if not green:
            return None, None, why
        _git(root, "add", "-A")
    plan["verification"] = {
        "mode": "product-bar" if product_bar else "scope-no-product-bar",
        "paths": _git(root, "diff", "--cached", "--name-only").splitlines(),
        "result": "pass",
    }
    return closed, plan, None


def _apply_approval(root, candidate, plan):
    statuses = plan.get("statuses") or {}
    for rel, rows in statuses.items():
        live = Path(root) / rel
        refusal = intake._rewrite_toml_statuses(live, rel, list(rows))
        if refusal:
            return refusal
    if statuses or plan.get("approves"):
        # Snapshot prose carries a date. Bind it to the reviewed candidate's
        # commit date so completion replay tomorrow derives identical bytes.
        publication_day = _git(root, "show", "-s", "--format=%cs", candidate)
        clock = baseline_snapshot._today
        try:
            baseline_snapshot._today = lambda: publication_day
            approves = plan.get("approves") or {}
            baseline_snapshot.copy_live(root, approves=approves, exact_rels=approves)
        except SystemExit as exc:
            return "snapshot copy refused: {}".format(exc)
        finally:
            baseline_snapshot._today = clock
    return None


def _outside_adjudication_scope(root):
    changed = _git(root, "diff", "--cached", "--name-only").splitlines()
    allowed = (
        list(publication_verify.ADJUDICATION_SURFACES)
        + [baseline_snapshot.SNAPSHOT_DIR + "/", "docs/id-watermark"]
        + publication_verify.generated_paths(Path(root))
    )
    return not all(
        any(
            p == a.rstrip("/") or (a.endswith("/") and p.startswith(a)) for a in allowed
        )
        for p in changed
    )


def derive_tree(root, turn, candidate="HEAD", tier="all", *, import_objects=True):
    """Independently replay the exact publication and return its tree and plan."""
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    try:
        cand = _git(root, "rev-parse", candidate)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="primary-publication-") as td:
            scratch = Path(td) / "repo"
            _git(root, "clone", "--shared", "--no-checkout", str(root), str(scratch))
            _git(scratch, "checkout", "--detach", cand)
            closed, plan, refusal = _project(scratch, turn, cand, tier, root)
            if refusal:
                return None, None, None, refusal
            tree = _git(scratch, "write-tree")
            if import_objects:
                # Import newly-written blobs/tree before the disposable object store
                # disappears. A temporary commit makes the object closure fetchable;
                # no primary ref is advanced.
                carrier = _git(
                    scratch, "commit-tree", tree, "-p", cand, "-m", "projection carrier"
                )
                _git(root, "fetch", "--no-tags", str(scratch), carrier)
        return tree, closed, plan, None
    except (KeyError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
        return None, None, None, str(exc)


def publish(root, turn, candidate="HEAD", tier="all"):
    """Replay, verify and atomically commit one publication; lock is caller-held."""
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    cand = None
    installed = False
    try:
        cand = _git(root, "rev-parse", candidate)
        if cand != _git(root, "rev-parse", "HEAD") or _git(
            root, "status", "--porcelain"
        ):
            return None, "primary is not clean at the exact candidate"
        primary_turn.validate(root, turn, cand)
        tree, closed, plan, refusal = derive_tree(root, turn, cand, tier)
        if refusal:
            return None, refusal
        _git(root, "read-tree", "--reset", "-u", tree)
        installed = True
        if _git(root, "write-tree") != tree:
            raise ValueError("installed projection differs from verified tree")
        author = (plan or {}).get("author") or {}
        if not author:
            raise ValueError("publication has no verified adjudicator author")
        env = dict(
            __import__("os").environ,
            GIT_AUTHOR_NAME=author["name"],
            GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL=author["email"],
        )
        msg = "adjudicate: {} primary publication\n\n{}{}\nPublication-Candidate: {}\nPublication-Tree: {}".format(
            turn["wi"], TRAILER, turn["turn"], cand, tree
        )
        msg += "\nPublication-Verification: " + json.dumps(
            plan["verification"], sort_keys=True
        )
        p = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(root), "commit", "-m", msg],
            env=env,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        )
        if p.returncode:
            _git(root, "reset", "--hard", cand)
            installed = False
            return None, "publication commit failed: " + (p.stdout + p.stderr)[-2000:]
        if _git(root, "rev-parse", "HEAD^{tree}") != tree:
            raise ValueError("publication commit tree differs from the verified tree")
        return closed, None
    except (KeyError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
        if installed and cand:
            try:
                _git(root, "reset", "--hard", cand)
            except RuntimeError as restore:
                return None, "{}; primary restore failed: {}".format(exc, restore)
        return None, str(exc)


def publication_commit(root, turn):
    """Verified reachable publication carrier for a durable turn, else None."""
    token = TRAILER + str(turn.get("turn", ""))
    out = _git(
        root, "log", "--format=%H", "--fixed-strings", "--grep=" + token, check=False
    )
    return next(
        (rev for rev in out.splitlines() if _verified_candidate(root, turn, rev)), None
    )


def _verified_candidate(root, turn, rev):
    """One exact carrier proof shared by completion and governing identity."""
    token = TRAILER + str(turn.get("turn", ""))
    proof = (str(Path(root).resolve()), rev, json.dumps(turn, sort_keys=True))
    if proof in _VERIFIED_PUBLICATIONS:
        return _git(root, "rev-parse", rev + "^1")
    body = _git(root, "show", "-s", "--format=%B", rev)
    if [line for line in body.splitlines() if line.startswith(TRAILER)] != [token]:
        return None
    tree = _git(root, "show", "-s", "--format=%T", rev)
    match = re.search(r"(?m)^Publication-Candidate: ([0-9a-f]{40})$", body)
    if not match:
        return None
    if _git(root, "show", "-s", "--format=%P", rev) != match.group(1):
        return None
    expected, _closed, plan, refusal = derive_tree(
        root, turn, match.group(1), import_objects=False
    )
    if refusal or expected != tree:
        return None
    receipt = "Publication-Verification: " + json.dumps(
        plan["verification"], sort_keys=True
    )
    if receipt not in body.splitlines():
        return None
    author = _git(root, "show", "-s", "--format=%an%x00%ae", rev).split("\0")
    wanted = (plan or {}).get("author") or {}
    if not wanted or author != [wanted.get("name"), wanted.get("email")]:
        return None
    spec = Path(str(turn.get("spec", ""))).name
    homes = ("docs/work/complete/" + spec, "docs/archive/work/complete/" + spec)
    if any(
        _git(root, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", rev, "--", home) for home in homes
    ):
        _VERIFIED_PUBLICATIONS.add(proof)
        return _git(root, "rev-parse", rev + "^1")
    return None


def carrier_candidate(root, rev):
    """Read-only mapping of this exact verified publication, never a reset peel."""
    try:
        rev, body = _git(root, "show", "-s", "--format=%H%x00%B", rev).split("\0", 1)
        matches = re.findall(
            r"(?m)^Primary-Publication-Turn: (WI-[0-9]+-[0-9a-f]{40})$", body
        )
        if len(matches) != 1:
            return None
        path = primary_turn.RECORDS + "/" + matches[0] + "/turn.toml"
        turn = primary_turn.parse_record(_git(root, "show", rev + ":" + path), path)
        return _verified_candidate(root, turn, rev)
    except (KeyError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
        return None


def completed(root, turn):
    return publication_commit(root, turn) is not None


def pending(root):
    """One unfinished committed selection, or None; malformed/duplicate refuses."""
    root = Path(root)
    found = []
    for path in sorted((root / primary_turn.RECORDS).glob("*/turn.toml")):
        turn = primary_turn._read_record(root, path)
        if not completed(root, turn):
            found.append(turn)
    if len(found) > 1:
        raise ValueError(
            "multiple unfinished primary adjudications: "
            + ", ".join(turn["wi"] for turn in found)
        )
    return found[0] if found else None


def writer_refusal(root):
    """A durable pending turn owns the primary writer even between processes."""
    try:
        turn = pending(root)
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
        return "primary adjudication selection is unreadable: {}".format(exc)
    if turn:
        return "primary adjudication {} is pending; resume it before another writer".format(
            turn["wi"]
        )
    return None


class _CarrierReader:
    """Bind one verifier into the shared algorithm without reversing imports."""

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        value = getattr(verdict, name)
        if (
            inspect.isfunction(value)
            and "carrier" in inspect.signature(value).parameters
        ):
            return partial(value, carrier=carrier_candidate)
        return value


verdict_reader = _CarrierReader()

</file>

<file path="tests/test_primary_publication.py">
import os

import pytest
import sys

    SCRIPTS,

)

def git(root, *args):
    return subprocess.check_output(["git", "-C", str(root), *args], text=True).strip()


def repo(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "r"
    root.mkdir()
    git(root, "init", "-q")
    git(root, "config", "user.name", "Test")
    git(root, "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
    (root / "docs/work/queued").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "docs/work/queued/WI-1-x.md").write_text("x\n")
    git(root, "add", "-A")
    git(root, "commit", "-qm", "base")
    return root


def test_completion_rejects_restamped_foreign_tree(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    root = repo(tmp_path)
    base = git(root, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
    turn = {"turn": "WI-1-" + base, "spec": "docs/work/queued/WI-1-x.md"}
    (root / "docs/work/complete").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "docs/work/complete/WI-1-x.md").write_text("closed\n")
    (root / "foreign").write_text("smuggled\n")
    git(root, "add", "-A")
    tree = git(root, "write-tree")
    msg = "x\n\n{}{}\nPublication-Candidate: {}\nPublication-Tree: {}".format(
        pp.TRAILER, turn["turn"], base, tree
    )
    git(root, "commit", "-qm", msg)
    monkeypatch.setattr(pp, "derive_tree", lambda *a, **k: ("0" * 40, [], {}, None))
    assert pp.publication_commit(root, turn) is None


def test_completion_requires_candidate_as_first_parent(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    root = repo(tmp_path)
    base = git(root, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
    (root / "docs/work/complete").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "docs/work/complete/WI-1-x.md").write_text("closed\n")
    git(root, "add", "-A")
    git(root, "commit", "-qm", "other")
    tree = git(root, "show", "-s", "--format=%T", "HEAD")
    turn = {"turn": "WI-1-" + base, "spec": "docs/work/queued/WI-1-x.md"}
    msg = "x\n\n{}{}\nPublication-Candidate: {}\nPublication-Tree: {}".format(
        pp.TRAILER, turn["turn"], base, tree
    )
    git(root, "commit", "--allow-empty", "-qm", msg)
    monkeypatch.setattr(pp, "derive_tree", lambda *a, **k: (tree, [], {}, None))
    assert pp.publication_commit(root, turn) is None


def test_real_primary_close_moves_exact_queued_spec(tmp_path):
    root = repo(tmp_path)
    spec = root / "docs/work/queued/WI-1-x.md"
    spec.write_text(
        '+++\nid = "WI-1"\ntitle = "clean close"\nsafety_class = "adjudication"\nspecref = "x"\n+++\n\n## Context\n\njudge\n'
    )

    text = terminal.read_text()
